   343	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &process_count);
   344	
   345	    int num_points = 1000;
   346	    double threshold = 2.0;
   347	    int validate_flag = 0;
   348	    int print_results_flag = 0;
   349	    int parse_status = 0;
   350	
   351	    // Rank zero owns command-line diagnostics and distributes one canonical
   352	    // configuration, avoiding duplicate output under mpirun.
   353	    if (rank == 0) {
   354	        for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   355	            if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   356	                num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
   357	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   358	                threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
   359	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   360	                validate_flag = 1;
   361	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   362	                print_results_flag = 1;
   363	            } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   364	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   365	                parse_status = 2;
   366	                break;
   367	            } else {
   368	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   369	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   370	                parse_status = 1;
   371	                break;
   372	            }
   373	        }
   374	
   375	        if (parse_status == 0 && (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0)) {
   376	            printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n",
   377	                   num_points, threshold);
   378	            parse_status = 1;
   379	        }
   380	    }
   381	
   382	    MPI_Bcast(&parse_status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   383	    if (parse_status != 0) {
   384	        MPI_Finalize();
   385	        return parse_status == 2 ? 0 : 1;
   386	    }
   387	
   388	    int integer_options[3] = {num_points, validate_flag, print_results_flag};
   389	    MPI_Bcast(integer_options, 3, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   390	    MPI_Bcast(&threshold, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   391	    num_points = integer_options[0];
   392	    validate_flag = integer_options[1];
   393	    print_results_flag = integer_options[2];
   394	
   395	    if (rank == 0) {
   396	        printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
   397	        printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
   398	        printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
   399	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate_flag ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   400	    }
   401	    
   402	    // Deterministic local generation avoids distributing the replicated point
   403	    // array and produces byte-identical input on every rank.
   404	    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
   405	    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
   406	    
   407	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   408	    const double cluster_start = MPI_Wtime();
   409	    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(
   410	        points, threshold, rank, process_count, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   411	    const double local_cluster_time = MPI_Wtime() - cluster_start;
   412	
   413	    double cluster_time_seconds = 0.0;
   414	    MPI_Reduce(&local_cluster_time, &cluster_time_seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE,
   415	               MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   416	
   417	    int exit_code = 0;
   418	    if (rank == 0) {
   419	        const long cluster_time_milliseconds =
   420	            static_cast<long>(cluster_time_seconds * 1000.0);
   421	        printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time_milliseconds);
   422	        printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
   423	    
   424	        int total_clustered = 0;
   425	        int max_cluster_size = 0;
   426	        for (const Cluster& cluster : clusters) {
   427	            const int size = static_cast<int>(cluster.members.size());
   428	            total_clustered += size;
   429	            max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
   430	        }
   431	
   432	        const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :
   433	            static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
   434	
   435	        printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n",
   436	               total_clustered, num_points,
   437	               100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
   438	        printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
   439	        printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
   440	
   441	        const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / cluster_time_seconds;
   442	        const double points_per_sec = num_points / cluster_time_seconds;
   443	        printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n",
   444	               clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
   445	
   446	        if (print_results_flag) {
   447	            std::vector<double> membership_data;
   448	            membership_data.reserve(num_points);
   449	            std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
   450	            for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   451	                for (const int member : clusters[c].members) {
   452	                    membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
   453	                }
   454	            }
   455	            for (const int membership_index : membership) {
   456	                membership_data.push_back(static_cast<double>(membership_index));
   457	            }
   458	            print_results(membership_data, "ClusterMembership");
   459	        }
   460	
   461	        if (validate_flag) {
   462	            const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
   463	            printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   464	            exit_code = valid ? 0 : 1;
   465	        }
   466	    }
   467	
   468	    MPI_Bcast(&exit_code, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   469	    MPI_Finalize();
   470	    return exit_code;
   471	}

+inline double distanceSquared(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
+    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
+    const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
+    return dx * dx + dy * dy;
+}
+
+// Scratch storage is retained across seeds, avoiding allocation in the hot path.
+// A point that reaches the threshold can be dropped permanently from a candidate:
+// its maximum distance can only increase as members are added.
+class CandidateWorkspace {
+public:
+    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
+        : max_distance_squared_(point_count) {
+        eligible_.reserve(point_count);
+        members.reserve(point_count);
+    }
+
+    int generate(const int seed_point,
+                 const std::vector<int>& unclustered,
+                 const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                 const double threshold_squared) {
+        eligible_.clear();
+        members.clear();
+        members.push_back(seed_point);
+
+        int closest_point = -1;
+        double min_diameter_squared = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+
+        // Build the seed's feasible set in point-index order.  Strict comparisons
+        // retain the sequential implementation's lower-index tie breaking.
+        for (const int candidate : unclustered) {
+            if (candidate == seed_point) continue;
+
+            const double diameter_squared =
+                distanceSquared(points[candidate], points[seed_point]);
+            if (diameter_squared < threshold_squared) {
+                max_distance_squared_[candidate] = diameter_squared;
+                eligible_.push_back(candidate);
+                if (diameter_squared < min_diameter_squared) {
+                    min_diameter_squared = diameter_squared;
+                    closest_point = candidate;
+                }
+            }
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        while (closest_point >= 0) {
+            members.push_back(closest_point);
+
+            int next_closest = -1;
+            double next_min_diameter_squared =
+                std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+            size_t output = 0;
+
+            // Incrementally update each point's diameter and compact away both
+            // the selected point and points that can never again be feasible.
+            for (const int candidate : eligible_) {
+                if (candidate == closest_point) continue;
+
+                const double new_distance_squared =
+                    distanceSquared(points[candidate], points[closest_point]);
+                const double diameter_squared =
+                    std::max(max_distance_squared_[candidate], new_distance_squared);
+
+                if (diameter_squared < threshold_squared) {
+                    max_distance_squared_[candidate] = diameter_squared;
+                    eligible_[output++] = candidate;
+                    if (diameter_squared < next_min_diameter_squared) {
+                        next_min_diameter_squared = diameter_squared;
+                        next_closest = candidate;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            eligible_.resize(output);
+            closest_point = next_closest;
         }
+
+        return static_cast<int>(members.size());
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
-}

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelization while preserving the `qtclustering` executable name.

Key changes:

- Cyclic distribution of seed evaluations across MPI ranks.
- Deterministic global winner selection using `MPI_MAXLOC`.
- Broadcasts only the winning cluster each iteration.
- Incremental squared-diameter tracking and infeasible-candidate pruning.
- Root-only reporting and validation.
- Required MPI integration in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt:9).
- MPI algorithm and initialization in [qtclustering.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp:173).

Validation passed across 1–16 ranks. Result hashes exactly matched the original for all tested sizes and thresholds.

Performance at 1,000 points:

- Original sequential: 7,011 ms
- MPI code, 1 rank: 86 ms
- 2 ranks: 45 ms
- 4 ranks: 27 ms
- 8 ranks: 16 ms

AddressSanitizer and oversubscription tests also passed.

+inline double distanceSquared(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
+    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
+    const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
+    return dx * dx + dy * dy;
+}
+
+// Scratch storage is retained across seeds, avoiding allocation in the hot path.
+// A point that reaches the threshold can be dropped permanently from a candidate:
+// its maximum distance can only increase as members are added.
+class CandidateWorkspace {
+public:
+    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
+        : max_distance_squared_(point_count) {
+        eligible_.reserve(point_count);
+        members.reserve(point_count);
+    }
+
+    int generate(const int seed_point,
+                 const std::vector<int>& unclustered,
+                 const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                 const double threshold_squared) {
+        eligible_.clear();
+        members.clear();
+        members.push_back(seed_point);
+
+        int closest_point = -1;
+        double min_diameter_squared = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+
+        // Build the seed's feasible set in point-index order.  Strict comparisons
+        // retain the sequential implementation's lower-index tie breaking.
+        for (const int candidate : unclustered) {
+            if (candidate == seed_point) continue;
+
+            const double diameter_squared =
+                distanceSquared(points[candidate], points[seed_point]);
+            if (diameter_squared < threshold_squared) {
+                max_distance_squared_[candidate] = diameter_squared;
+                eligible_.push_back(candidate);
+                if (diameter_squared < min_diameter_squared) {
+                    min_diameter_squared = diameter_squared;
+                    closest_point = candidate;
+                }
+            }
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        while (closest_point >= 0) {
+            members.push_back(closest_point);
+
+            int next_closest = -1;
+            double next_min_diameter_squared =
+                std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+            size_t output = 0;
+
+            // Incrementally update each point's diameter and compact away both
+            // the selected point and points that can never again be feasible.
+            for (const int candidate : eligible_) {
+                if (candidate == closest_point) continue;
+
+                const double new_distance_squared =
+                    distanceSquared(points[candidate], points[closest_point]);
+                const double diameter_squared =
+                    std::max(max_distance_squared_[candidate], new_distance_squared);
+
+                if (diameter_squared < threshold_squared) {
+                    max_distance_squared_[candidate] = diameter_squared;
+                    eligible_[output++] = candidate;
+                    if (diameter_squared < next_min_diameter_squared) {
+                        next_min_diameter_squared = diameter_squared;
+                        next_closest = candidate;
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+
+            eligible_.resize(output);
+            closest_point = next_closest;
         }
+
+        return static_cast<int>(members.size());
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
-}

Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelization while preserving the `qtclustering` executable name.

Key changes:

- Cyclic distribution of seed evaluations across MPI ranks.
- Deterministic global winner selection using `MPI_MAXLOC`.
- Broadcasts only the winning cluster each iteration.
- Incremental squared-diameter tracking and infeasible-candidate pruning.
- Root-only reporting and validation.
- Required MPI integration in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt:9).
- MPI algorithm and initialization in [qtclustering.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r2/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp:173).

Validation passed across 1–16 ranks. Result hashes exactly matched the original for all tested sizes and thresholds.

- Original sequential: 7,011 ms
- MPI code, 1 rank: 86 ms
- 2 ranks: 45 ms
- 4 ranks: 27 ms
- 8 ranks: 16 ms
